> ├── app/                    ← Next.js App Router (layout, page, globals.css)
> ├── components/             ← React UI Components (18 components)
> │   ├── ui/                 ← Base UI (button, card)
> │   ├── executive-dashboard

> ├── lib/                    ← Business Logic (14 modules)
> │   ├── asset-numbering.ts
> │   ├── domain.mjs
> │   ├── lifecycle.mjs
> │   ├── master-data.mjs
> │   ├── planning.ts

> │   ├── procurement-flow.ts

> │   ├── executive-dashboard.ts

> │   ├── csv.mjs
> │   ├── seed.ts
> │   ├── storage.ts
> │   ├── users.mjs
> │   ├── system-log.ts
> │   ├── workspace-menu.ts
> │   └── data/asset-catalog.json
> ├── backend/                ← Separated backend server
> │   ├── src/server.ts
> │   ├── src/policy.ts
> │   └── .env.example
> ├── prisma/                 ← Database
> │   ├── schema.prisma       ← Main schema
> │   ├── phase2.prisma       ← Phase 2 schema
> │   ├── phase2-initial.sql
> │   ├── phase2-guards.sql
> │   ├── audit-guards.sql
> │   └── migrations/         ← 2 migrations (initial + audit guards)
> ├── docs/                   ← 9 documentation files
> │   ├── ACCOUNTING-POLICY.md
> │   ├── ASSET-NUMBERING.md
> │   ├── CATALOG-AND-EXCEL.md
> │   ├── CONNECTED-WORKFLOW.md

> │   ├── REGULATORY-BASELINE.md
> │   ├── SETTINGS-MASTERS.md
> │   └── SYSTEM-LOG.md
> ├── tests/                  ← 13 integration test suites
> ├── scripts/                ← prepare-offline.mjs, serve.mjs
> └── .openai/hosting.json    ← OpenAI hosting config
> ```
> 
> ---
> 
> ## 🎯 Business Domains (จาก docs + lib)
> 
> | Domain | Components | Logic | Tests |
> |--------|-----------|-------|-------|
> | **Asset Catalog & Numbering** | `asset-catalog-workspace`, `asset-numbering-workspace` | `asset-numbering.ts` | `asset-numbering.test.mjs` |
> | **Planning / Budgeting** | `planning-workspace`, `planning-round-detail` | `planning.ts`, `planning-pdf.ts` | `planning.test.mjs` |
> | **Procurement Flow** | `procurement-flow-workspace` | `procurement-flow.ts` | `procurement-flow.test.mjs` |
> | **Asset Lifecycle** | `lifecycle-workspace` | `lifecycle.mjs` | `lifecycle.test.mjs` |
> | **Master Data** | `master-data-management` | `master-data.mjs` | `master-data.test.mjs`, `master-parent.test.mjs` |
> | **Regulatory Compliance** | `regulatory-workspace` | `regulatory.ts` | `policy.test.mjs` |
> | **Executive Dashboard** | `executive-dashboard` | `executive-dashboard.ts` | `executive-dashboard.test.mjs` |
> | **User Management & Auth** | `user-management` | `users.mjs`, `backend/policy.ts` | `users.test.mjs`, `backend-auth.test.mjs` |
> | **System Logging / Audit** | `system-log-viewer`, `system-activity` | `system-log.ts`, `audit-guards.sql` | `system-log.test.mjs` |
> | **Excel/CSV Import** | `legacy-excel-import` | `excel-import.ts`, `csv.mjs` | `excel-import.test.mjs`, `csv.test.mjs` |
> | **Accounting / Depreciation** | `ops-depreciation-reference` | `domain.mjs` | `domain.test.mjs` |
> 
> ---
> 
> ## 🔄 Workflow (Connected Workflow)
> 
> ```
> ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────────┐
> │  Master Data │────▶│   Planning   │────▶│  Procurement Flow│
> │  (Catalog,   │     │  (Budget,    │     │  (Request →      │
> │   Numbering) │     │   Rounds)    │     │   Approve → Buy) │
> └──────────────┘     └──────────────┘     └──────────────────┘
>                                                        │
> ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐     ┌────────────▼───────┐
> │ Regulatory   │◀────│  Executive   │◀────│    Lifecycle       │
> │ (Policy,     │     │  Dashboard   │     │ (Deploy → Use →    │
> │  Compliance) │     │  (Reports)   │     │  Maintain → Dispose)│
> └──────────────┘     └──────────────┘     └────────────────────┘
>          │
>          ▼
> ┌──────────────────┐
> │ Audit / System   │
> │ Log              │
> └──────────────────┘
> ```
> 
> ---
> 
> ## 📊 Key Observations
> 
> ### ✅ Strengths
> 1. **Well-structured** — แยก frontend/backend/logic/db ชัดเจน
> 2. **Comprehensive docs** — 11+ documents ครอบคลุมทุก domain
> 3. **Solid testing** — 13 test suites ครอบคลุมทุก business logic
> 4. **Phased development** — Phase 1 + Phase 2 (มี `phase2.prisma`, `REDTEAM-PHASE2.md`)
> 5. **Audit-ready** — `audit-guards.sql`, `system-log`, `SYSTEM-LOG.md`
> 6. **Offline-capable** — `scripts/prepare-offline.mjs` + `serve.mjs`
> 7. **Excel/CSV import** — รองรับ legacy data migration
> 
> ### ⚠️ Areas to Watch
> 1. **Prisma dual schema** — `schema.prisma` + `phase2.prisma` → ต้องจัดการ migration ให้อยู่ตัว
> 2. **Backend separation** — `backend/` มี `server.ts` + `policy.ts` แยกจาก Next.js API routes → ต้อง sync state
> 3. **OpenAI hosting** — `.openai/hosting.json` → ดูเหมือน deploy บน OpenAI platform
> 4. **No Docker** — ยังไม่มี Dockerfile/docker-compose → deployment ขึ้นอยู่กับ environment
> 
> ---
> 
> ## 🚀 How to Run
> 
> ```bash
> # Install
> npm install
> 
> # Setup DB
> npx prisma migrate dev
> 
> # Seed data
> npm run seed  (ถ้ามี)
> 
> # Dev server
> npm run dev
> 
> # Tests
> node --test tests/
> 
> # Offline mode
> node scripts/prepare-offline.mjs
> node scripts/serve.mjs
> ```
> 
> ---
> 
> สรุปคือ **EDR-New** เป็น **Asset Management + Procurement + Planning System** ที่ครบวงจร ตั้งแต่:
> - จัด asset catalog + numbering
> - วางแผนงบ (planning rounds)
> - จัดซื้อ (procurement flow)
> - Lifecycle management
> - Regulatory compliance
> - Executive dashboard
> - Audit trail
> 
> มีโครงสร้างเป็นระเบียบ มี docs ครบ มี tests ครอบคลุม เหมาะกับองค์กรที่ต้องบริหารสินทรัพย์/จัดซื้ออย่างเป็นระบบครับ 🎯

> ## 🚀 รันด้วยตัวเอง (Terminal)
> 
> เปิด **PowerShell / CMD** แล้วรันตามนี้:
> 
> ```powershell
> # 1. ไปที่โปรเจกต์
> cd C:\Working\edr-new
> 
> # 2. ติดตั้ง dependencies (ยังไม่มี node_modules)
> npm install
> 
> # 3. Setup database (ถ้ามี SQLite ไม่ต้องทำอะไร / ถ้า MySQL/Postgres ต้องตั้ง .env)
> npx prisma migrate dev
> npx prisma db seed
> 
> # 4. รัน dev server
> npm run dev
> ```
> 
> แล้วเปิด **http://localhost:3000** ใน browser
> 
> ---
> 
> ### ⚠️ ถ้าติดปัญหา ลองเช็ค:
> 
> | ปัญหา | แก้ |
> |--------|-----|
> | `npm install` ติด | ลบ `package-lock.json` แล้ว `npm install` ใหม่ |
> | Prisma migrate fail | เช็ค `prisma/schema.prisma` ว่า datasource URL ถูกต้อง |
> | Port 3000 ถูกใช้ | `npm run dev -- -p 3001` |
> | `backend/` ต้องรันแยก | `cd backend && npm install && npm run dev` |
> | อยากดู offline mode | `node scripts/prepare-offline.mjs` แล้ว `node scripts/serve.mjs` |
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 🔧 ถ้าอยากได้ Docker (อนาคต):
> 
> ```dockerfile
> # Dockerfile (ยังไม่มีในโปรเจกต์)
> FROM node:20-alpine
> WORKDIR /app
> COPY package*.json ./
> RUN npm ci
> COPY . .
> RUN npx prisma generate
> EXPOSE 3000
> CMD ["npm", "run", "dev"]
> ```
> 
> ---
> 
> ลองรันดูครับ แล้วถ้ามี error พากย์มาให้ผมช่วย debug ต่อได้เลย 💪

> ## 🔧 Workflow (Step-by-Step)

> ### Phase 1: Setup Connections (ครั้งเดียว)
> 
> ```powershell
> # ─── ON COMPUTER (Windows) ───
> 
> # 1. SSH ไป DGX (ตั้ง alias ใน .ssh/config)

> #        IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_rsa
> #        ServerAliveInterval 60
>

> 
> # 3. Port forward Database (ถ้า DB อยู่บน DGX)

> 
> # 4. หรือถ้า DB อยู่บน Computer → DGX access ผ่าน network

> ```
> 
> ```bash

> ## 🔐 Security Rules
> 
> | Rule | Why |
> |------|-----|
> | `.env` **ห้าม** commit | DB passwords, API keys |
> | SSH key-only (ไม่มี password) | DGX access |
> | GitHub 2FA | Protect source |
> | Database backup → encrypted |数据安全 |
> | AgentRoom `secrets.json` → chmod 600 | AI agents shouldn't leak keys |

#### _Assistant_

> The problem is clear: `Set-Content -Encoding UTF8` in Windows PowerShell adds a BOM (Byte Order Mark: `\357\273\277`) at the beginning of the file. SSH doesn't understand BOM. I need to write the file WITHOUT BOM.
> 
> In Windows PowerShell 5.1, `-Encoding UTF8` adds BOM. To write without BOM, I need to use .NET's `System.IO.File.WriteAllText` with `UTF8Encoding($false)`.
> 
> Let me fix this.

#### _Assistant_

> ## 🔴 ปัญหา: BOM (Byte Order Mark)
> 
> `Set-Content -Encoding UTF8` ใน Windows PowerShell **เติม BOM** (`\357\273\277`) → SSH ไม่อ่าน
> 
> ---
> 
> ## 🔧 แก้ — Write Without BOM
> 
> ```powershell
> $configPath = "$env:USERPROFILE\.ssh\config"
> 
> $content = @"
> Host dgx
>     HostName 192.168.1.224
>     User admin
>     ServerAliveInterval 60
> "@
> 
> # ใช้ .NET เขียน WITHOUT BOM
> [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($configPath, $content, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding $false))
> 
> # เช็คไฟล์ (ต้องไม่มี BOM)
> Get-Content $configPath
> 
> # ทดสอบ
> ssh dgx "hostname && whoami"
> ```
> 
> > พิมพ์ password: **Ppit@1234**
> 
> ---
>

> 
> ```powershell
> ssh dgx "cd ~/Working && git clone https://github.com/siravitha/edr-new.git && cd edr-new && npm install && echo 'REPO READY ✅'"
> ```

#### _Assistant_

> The user wants:
> 1. SQLite instead of MySQL
> 2. SSH key for GitHub (option 1)
> 
> Let me give them clear commands to:
> 1. Change Prisma schema from MySQL to SQLite
> 2. Set DATABASE_URL for SQLite
> 3. Run prisma migrate + seed
> 4. Get their SSH public key to add to GitHub
> 5. Push to GitHub
> 6. Fix and run tests

#### _Assistant_

> ## 🚀SQLite + GitHub SSH — Copy-Paste ทั้งหมด
> 
> ```powershell
> cd C:\Working\edr-new
> 
> # ═══════════════════════════════════════════
> # A: DATABASE → SQLite
> # ═══════════════════════════════════════════
> Write-Host "═══ A: SQLite ═══" -ForegroundColor Cyan
> 
> # 1. แก้ schema.prisma (MySQL → SQLite)
> $schema = Get-Content "prisma\schema.prisma" -Raw
> $schema = $schema -replace 'provider\s*=\s*"mysql"', 'provider = "sqlite"'
> $schema = $schema -replace 'url\s*=\s*env\("DATABASE_URL"\)', 'url      = "file:./dev.db"'
> [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText("$PWD\prisma\schema.prisma", $schema, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding $false))
> 
> # 2. สร้าง .env (SQLite ไม่ต้องการ URL แต่ Prisma ยังอ่าน)
> [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText("$PWD\.env", "DATABASE_URL=""file:./dev.db""`n", (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding $false))
> 
> # 3. Generate + Migrate + Seed
> npx prisma generate
> npx prisma migrate dev --name init_sqlite
> npx prisma db seed
> 
> Write-Host "✅ Database ready!" -ForegroundColor Green
> 
> # ═══════════════════════════════════════════
> # C: GITHUB SSH KEY
> # ═══════════════════════════════════════════
> Write-Host "`n═══ C: GITHUB ═══" -ForegroundColor Cyan
> 
> # Copy public key (paste ไป GitHub)
> $pubKey = Get-Content "$env:USERPROFILE\.ssh\id_ed25519.pub"
> Write-Host "`n📋 Copy key นี้ → https://github.com/settings/ssh/new" -ForegroundColor Yellow
> Write-Host $pubKey -ForegroundColor White
> Write-Host "═══════════════════════════════════════════" -ForegroundColor Yellow
> 
> # รอยืนยันว่า add แล้ว
> Write-Host "`nPress ENTER after adding key to GitHub..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
> Read-Host
> 
> # ทดสอบ GitHub SSH
> ssh -T git@github.com
> # → ถ้าเห็น "Hi siravitha! You've successfully authenticated..." = ✅
> 
> # Push
> git remote set-url origin git@github.com:siravitha/edr-new.git
> git add .
> git commit -m "chore: switch to SQLite, initial setup"
> git push origin main
> 
> # ═══════════════════════════════════════════
> # F: TESTS (แก้ syntax)
> # ═══════════════════════════════════════════
> Write-Host "`n═══ F: TESTS ═══" -ForegroundColor Cyan
> Get-ChildItem tests/*.test.mjs | ForEach-Object {
>     Write-Host "  Running: $($_.Name)" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
>     node --test $_.FullName 2>&1 | Select-String "pass|fail|✖|✔"
> }
> 
> # ═══════════════════════════════════════════
> # RESTART DEV
> # ═══════════════════════════════════════════
> Write-Host "`n═══ RESTART ═══" -ForegroundColor Cyan
> npm run dev
> ```
> 
> ---
> 
> ## 📌 ลำดับที่ต้องทำ
> 
> ```
> 1. รัน script บน
> 2. เมื่อเห็น public key → COPY
> 3. เปิด https://github.com/settings/ssh/new
> 4. Paste key → Add
> 5. กลับ Terminal → Press ENTER
> 6. Script จะ push + test + restart อัตโนมัติ
> ```
>

> ## 🔧 Fix A: Schema → SQLite (ลบ @db.* ทุกตัว)
> 
> ```powershell
> cd C:\Working\edr-new
> 
> # แก้ schema.prisma — ลบ native types ทั้งหมด
> $schema = Get-Content "prisma\schema.prisma" -Raw
> 
> # ลบ @db.Char(*)
> $schema = $schema -replace '\s*@db\.Char\(\d+\)', ''
> # ลบ @db.VarChar(*)
> $schema = $schema -replace '\s*@db\.VarChar\(\d+\)', ''
> # ลบ @db.Decimal(*)
> $schema = $schema -replace '\s*@db\.Decimal\(\d+,\s*\d+\)', ''
> # ลบ @db.DateTime(*)
> $schema = $schema -replace '\s*@db\.DateTime\(\d+\)', ''
> # ลบ @db.Text ถ้ามี
> $schema = $schema -replace '\s*@db\.Text', ''
> 
> # ยืนยัน provider = sqlite
> $schema = $schema -replace 'provider\s*=\s*"mysql"', 'provider = "sqlite"'
> # ยืนยัน url
> if ($schema -notmatch 'file:') {
>     $schema = $schema -replace 'url\s*=\s*env\("DATABASE_URL"\)', 'url      = "file:./dev.db"'
> }
> 
> [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText("$PWD\prisma\schema.prisma", $schema, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding $false))
> 
> Write-Host "✅ Schema cleaned!" -ForegroundColor Green
> Get-Content "prisma\schema.prisma" | Select-Object -First 30
> 
> # Migrate
> npx prisma generate
> npx prisma migrate dev --name init
> npx prisma db seed
> ```

#### _Assistant_

> Now I can see the root cause of all the test failures. There are 2 distinct issues:
> 
> 1. **6 tests** (asset-numbering, planning, procurement-flow, system-log, excel-import, executive-dashboard) → `ERR_UNKNOWN_FILE_EXTENSION: Unknown file extension ".ts"` — Node.js v22.11.0 can't import `.ts` files directly. These tests import TypeScript files from `lib/` (e.g., `lib/asset-numbering.ts`, `lib/planning-pdf.ts`, etc.)
> 
> 2. **1 test** (policy) → `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND: Cannot find module 'backend/dist/policy.js'` — The backend hasn't been compiled (no `dist/` folder)
> 
> The fix:
> 1. For the `.ts` import issue: Node.js v22.11.0 does NOT have native TypeScript support (that came in Node 23+/22.6+ with `--experimental-strip-types`). We need to either:
>    - Use `node --experimental-strip-types --test` flag (Node 22.6+)
>    - Or compile the TS files first
>    - Or use `tsx` as the test runner
> 
> Actually wait - Node.js v22.11.0 should support `--experimental-strip-types` flag since 22.6.0. Let me check...
> 
> Actually, `--experimental-strip-types` was added in Node 22.6.0. So Node 22.11.0 should have it. The fix is to run tests with:
> ```
> node --experimental-strip-types --test tests/*.test.mjs
> ```
> 
> Or alternatively, use `tsx` which handles TS natively:
> ```
> npx tsx --test tests/*.test.mjs
> ```
> 
> 2. For the policy test: Need to build the backend first:
> ```
> cd backend && npm run build
> ```
> Or the test should import from `backend/src/policy.ts` instead of `backend/dist/policy.js`
> 
> Let me give clear fixes.

> ## 📝 Feature 2: Asset Registration Form (CRUD)
> 
> ### Step 1: API Routes
> 
> ```typescript
> // C:\Working\edr-new\app\api\assets\route.ts
> import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
> import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
> 
> const prisma = new PrismaClient();
> 
> // GET /api/assets — List with pagination + filter
> export async function GET(request: Request) {
>   try {
>     const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
>     const page = parseInt(searchParams.get('page') || '1');
>     const pageSize = parseInt(searchParams.get('pageSize') || '20');
>     const search = searchParams.get('search') || '';
>     const status = searchParams.get('status') || '';
>     const department = searchParams.get('department') || '';
> 
>     const where: any = {};
>     if (search) {
>       where.OR = [
>         { assetNo: { contains: search } },
>         { name: { contains: search } },
>       ];
>     }
>     if (status) where.status = status;
>     if (department) where.department = department;
> 
>     const [assets, total] = await Promise.all([
>       prisma.asset.findMany({
>         where,
>         orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
>         skip: (page - 1) * pageSize,
>         take: pageSize,
>       }),
>       prisma.asset.count({ where }),
>     ]);
> 
>     return NextResponse.json({
>       success: true,
>       data: assets,
>       pagination: { page, pageSize, total, totalPages: Math.ceil(total / pageSize) },
>     });
>   } catch (error: any) {
>     return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
>   }
> }
> 
> // POST /api/assets — Create
> export async function POST(request: Request) {
>   try {
>     const body = await request.json();
>     const { assetNo, name, department, cost, quantity, purchaseDate, notes } = body;
> 
>     if (!assetNo || !name) {
>       return NextResponse.json(
>         { success: false, error: 'assetNo and name are required' },
>         { status: 400 }
>       );
>     }
> 
>     const asset = await prisma.asset.create({
>       data: {
>         assetNo,
>         name,
>         department: department || '',
>         cost: cost ? new Decimal(cost) : new Decimal(0),
>         quantity: quantity || 1,
>         purchaseDate: purchaseDate ? new Date(purchaseDate) : new Date(),
>         notes: notes || null,
>         status: 'ACTIVE',
>       },
>     });
> 
>     return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: asset }, { status: 201 });
>   } catch (error: any) {
>     return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
>   }
> }
> ```
> 
> ```typescript
> // C:\Working\edr-new\app\api\assets\[id]\route.ts
> import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
> import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
> 
> const prisma = new PrismaClient();
> 
> // PUT /api/assets/:id — Update
> export async function PUT(
>   request: Request,
>   { params }: { params: { id: string } }
> ) {
>   try {
>     const body = await request.json();
>     const { id } = params;
> 
>     const updateData: any = {};
>     const allowedFields = ['name', 'department', 'cost', 'quantity', 'purchaseDate', 'notes', 'status'];
>     for (const field of allowedFields) {
>       if (body[field] !== undefined) {
>         updateData[field] = field === 'cost' ? new Decimal(body[field]) :
>                            field === 'purchaseDate' ? new Date(body[field]) :
>                            body[field];
>       }
>     }
> 
>     const asset = await prisma.asset.update({ where: { id }, data: updateData });
>     return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: asset });
>   } catch (error: any) {
>     return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
>   }
> }
> 
> // DELETE /api/assets/:id
> export async function DELETE(
>   request: Request,
>   { params }: { params: { id: string } }
> ) {
>   try {
>     await prisma.asset.delete({ where: { id: params.id } });
>     return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: 'Asset deleted' });
>   } catch (error: any) {
>     return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
>   }
> }
> ```
>

> import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
> import { prisma } from "../../../lib/prisma";
> 
> export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
>   const params = request.nextUrl.searchParams;
>   const page = Math.max(1, Number(params.get("page") || 1));
>   const pageSize = Math.min(100, Number(params.get("pageSize") || 25));
>   const search = (params.get("search") || "").toLowerCase();
>   const status = params.get("status") || "";
>   const department = params.get("department") || "";
>   const hospitalId = params.get("hospitalId") || "";
>   const fiscalYear = params.get("fiscalYear") || "";
> 
>   const where: Record<string, unknown> = {};
>   if (hospitalId) where.hospitalId = hospitalId;
>   if (status) where.status = status;
>   if (department) where.department = department;
>   if (fiscalYear) where.fiscalYear = fiscalYear;
>   if (search) {
>     where.OR = [
>       { assetNumber: { contains: search } },
>       { assetName: { contains: search } },
>       { department: { contains: search } },
>       { location: { contains: search } },
>       { notes: { contains: search } },
>     ];
>   }
> 
>   const [assets, total] = await Promise.all([
>     prisma.asset.findMany({ where, orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, skip: (page - 1) * pageSize, take: pageSize }),
>     prisma.asset.count({ where }),
>   ]);
> 
>   return NextResponse.json({ assets, total, page, pageSize, totalPages: Math.ceil(total / pageSize) });
> }
> 
> export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
>   const body = await request.json();
>   const { assetNumber, assetName, assetCategory, department, location, quantity = 1, unitPrice = 0, supplier, purchaseDate, fiscalYear, hospitalId, notes = "" } = body;
> 
>   if (!assetNumber || !assetName) {
>     return NextResponse.json({ error: "assetNumber and assetName are required" }, { status: 400 });
>   }
> 
>   const existing = await prisma.asset.findUnique({ where: { assetNumber } });
>   if (existing) {
>     return NextResponse.json({ error: `Asset number ${assetNumber} already exists` }, { status: 409 });
>   }
> 
>   const asset = await prisma.asset.create({
>     data: {
>       assetNumber, assetName, assetCategory: assetCategory || "", department: department || "",
>       location: location || "", quantity, unitPrice, supplier: supplier || "",
>       purchaseDate: purchaseDate ? new Date(purchaseDate) : new Date(),
>       fiscalYear: fiscalYear || String(new Date().getFullYear() + 1),
>       hospitalId: hospitalId || "", notes, status: "ACTIVE",
>     },

>       fiscalYear: asset.fiscalYear, roundId: "", payload: JSON.stringify({ source: "manual" }), at: new Date(),

> **2) `C:\Working\edr-new\app\api\assets\[id]\route.ts`** (สร้างใหม่)
> 
> ```ts app/api/assets/[id]/route.ts
> import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
> import { prisma } from "../../../../lib/prisma";
> 
> interface Params { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
> 
> export async function GET(_request: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
>   const { id } = await params;
>   const asset = await prisma.asset.findUnique({ where: { id } });
>   if (!asset) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });
>   return NextResponse.json({ asset });
> }
> 
> export async function PUT(request: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
>   const { id } = await params;
>   const existing = await prisma.asset.findUnique({ where: { id } });
>   if (!existing) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });
> 
>   const body = await request.json();
>   const { assetName, assetCategory, department, location, quantity, unitPrice, supplier, purchaseDate, status, notes } = body;
> 
>   const asset = await prisma.asset.update({
>     where: { id },
>     data: {
>       assetName: assetName ?? existing.assetName,
>       assetCategory: assetCategory ?? existing.assetCategory,
>       department: department ?? existing.department,
>       location: location ?? existing.location,
>       quantity: quantity ?? existing.quantity,
>       unitPrice: unitPrice ?? existing.unitPrice,
>       supplier: supplier ?? existing.supplier,
>       purchaseDate: purchaseDate ? new Date(purchaseDate) : existing.purchaseDate,
>       status: status ?? existing.status,
>       notes: notes ?? existing.notes,
>     },
>   });
> 
>   await prisma.auditLog.create({
>     data: {
>       actor: "system", action: "ASSET_UPDATED", assetId: asset.id,
>       assetNumber: asset.assetNumber, hospitalId: asset.hospitalId,
>       fiscalYear: asset.fiscalYear, roundId: "",
>       payload: JSON.stringify({ changed: true }), at: new Date(),
>     },
>   });
> 
>   return NextResponse.json({ asset });
> }
> 
> export async function DELETE(_request: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
>   const { id } = await params;
>   const existing = await prisma.asset.findUnique({ where: { id } });
>   if (!existing) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });
> 
>   await prisma.auditLog.create({
>     data: {
>       actor: "system", action: "ASSET_DELETED", assetId: existing.id,
>       assetNumber: existing.assetNumber, hospitalId: existing.hospitalId,
>       fiscalYear: existing.fiscalYear, roundId: "",
>       payload: JSON.stringify({ deleted: true }), at: new Date(),
>     },
>   });
> 
>   await prisma.asset.delete({ where: { id } });
>   return NextResponse.json({ deleted: true });
> }
> ```

>                             <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="text-red-500" onClick={() => handleDelete(asset.id, asset.assetName)}>ลบ</Button>
>                           </div>
>                         </td>
>                       </tr>
>                     ))}
>                   </tbody>
>                 </table>
>               </div>
> 
>               {/* Pagination */}
>               {totalPages > 1 && (
>                 <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-4">
>                   <Button variant="outline" size="sm" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage(p => p - 1)}>← ก่อนหน้า</Button>
>                   <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">หน้า {page} จาก {totalPages}</span>
>                   <Button variant="outline" size="sm" disabled={page >= totalPages} onClick={() => setPage(p => p + 1)}>ถัดไป →</Button>
>                 </div>
>               )}
>             </>
>           )}
>         </CardContent>
>       </Card>
> 
>       {/* Create/Edit Modal */}
>       {showForm && (
>         <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 p-4" onClick={() => setShowForm(false)}>
>           <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow-xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
>             <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-bold">{editing ? "แก้ไขสินทรัพย์" : "เพิ่มสินทรัพย์"}</h2>
>             <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
>               <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols

> function openDatabase(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {>

>     let blocked = false;> 
>     const request = indexedDB.open(DATABASE, 3);> 
>     request.onupgradeneeded = () => {> 
>       if (!request.result.objectStoreNames.contains("state")) request.result.createObjectStore("state");> 
>       if (!request.result.objectStoreNames.contains("planningFiles")) request.result.createObjectStore("planningFiles");> 
>       if (!request.result.objectStoreNames.contains("systemLogs")) request.result.createObjectStore("systemLogs", {keyPath:"sequence",autoIncrement:true});> 
>       if (!request.result.objectStoreNames.contains("systemLogMeta")) request.result.createObjectStore("systemLogMeta");> 
>     };> 
>     request.onblocked = () => { blocked = true; reject(new Error("กรุณาปิดแท็บระบบอื่นก่อนอัปเกรดที่เก็บเอกสาร")); };> 
>     request.onsuccess = () => { if(blocked){request.result.close();return;} request.result.onversionchange = () => request.result.close(); resolve(request.result); };> 
>     request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);> 
>   });> 
> }> 
> export async function loadState(): Promise<DemoState> {> 
>   const db = await openDatabase();> 
>   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {> 
>     const tx = db.transaction("state", "readonly");> 
>     const request = tx.objectStore("state").get("current");> 
>     let value: DemoState;>

#### _Assistant_

> Now I see the issue! The schema has `output = "../backend/node_modules/.prisma/client"` which means the Prisma client is generated to the backend folder, NOT the root. So the root `node_modules/.prisma/client` has stale files.
> 
> I have two options:
> 1. Change the schema output to the root `node_modules/.prisma/client`
> 2. Remove the output line so it defaults to the node_modules of the project where prisma is installed
> 
> Since our Next.js app is at the root and uses `@prisma/client` from root `node_modules`, I should change the output to point to the root. Or better, just remove the custom output line so it defaults to the root `node_modules/.prisma/client`.
> 
> But wait - the backend also uses Prisma. Let me check if the backend has its own prisma setup.
> 
> Actually, looking at this more carefully:
> - The root has `@prisma/client` in package.json
> - The backend also has `@prisma/client` in its own package.json
> - The schema outputs to `../backend/node_modules/.prisma/client`
> 
> This means the root's `@prisma/client` package is just a wrapper that looks for `.prisma/client` in the root `node_modules`, but the actual generated client is in the backend. That's why it fails.
> 
> The simplest fix: change the output in schema.prisma to point to the root, or remove the output line entirely (which defaults to `node_modules/.prisma/client` relative to where prisma is run).
> 
> Let me change the output to `../node_modules/.prisma/client` (relative from prisma/ folder = root).

> export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
>   const params = request.nextUrl.searchParams;
>   const page = Math.max(1, Number(params.get("page") || 1));
>   const pageSize = Math.min(100, Number(params.get("pageSize") || 25));
>   const search = (params.get("search") || "").toLowerCase();
>   const status = params.get("status") || "";
>   const wardId = params.get("wardId") || "";
>   const hospitalId = params.get("hospitalId") || "";
> 
>   const where: Record<string, unknown> = {};
>   if (hospitalId) where.hospitalId = hospitalId;
>   if (wardId) where.wardId = wardId;
>   if (status) where.status = status;
>   if (search) {
>     where.OR = [
>       { assetNo: { contains: search } },
>       { name: { contains: search } },
>     ];
>   }
> 
>   const [assets, total] = await Promise.all([
>     prisma.asset.findMany({
>       where,
>       orderBy: { id: "desc" },
>       skip: (page - 1) * pageSize,
>       take: pageSize,
>     }),
>     prisma.asset.count({ where }),
>   ]);
> 
>   return NextResponse.json({ assets, total, page, pageSize, totalPages: Math.ceil(total / pageSize) });
> }

> ## Prompt สำหรับ Feature 3 (paste ทั้งก้อน)
> 
> ```
> อ่าน AGENTS.md และ .continue/rules/project-context.md ก่อน
> แล้วแก้ไฟล์ components/asset-catalog-workspace.tsx ให้ครบทุกข้อต่อไปนี้:
> 
> 1. DEBOUNCED SEARCH:
>    - เพิ่ม input search (useState + useEffect debounce 300ms)
>    - onChange → เก็บ keyword ใน state
>    - debounce แล้ว → fetch GET /api/assets?search={keyword}&page={page}
>    - placeholder: "ค้นหา asset code หรือชื่อ..."
> 
> 2. STATUS FILTER:
>    - เพิ่ม <select> dropdown
>    - options: "", "AVAILABLE", "IN_USE", "REPAIR", "QUARANTINED", "DISPOSED"
>    - label: "ทั้งหมด", "พร้อมใช้", "กำลังใช้", "ซ่อม", "กักกัน", "เลิกใช้"
>    - onChange → fetch /api/assets?status={value}&page=1
> 
> 3. WARD FILTER:
>    - เพิ่ม <select> dropdown (wardId)
>    - options: "", "W1", "W2", "W3", "W4", "W5"
>    - label: "ทุกหอ", "หอ 1"-"หอ 5"
>    - onChange → fetch /api/assets?wardId={value}&page=1
> 
> 4. PAGINATION:
>    - แสดง "แสดง 1-25 จาก 120 รายการ"
>    - ปุ่ม: [«] [หน้าก่อน] [หน้าถัดไป] [»]
>    - page state (เริ่มที่ 1)
>    - totalPages จาก response
>    - disabled ปุ่มเมื่อ page=1 หรือ page=totalPages
>    - คลิก → fetch /api/assets?page={newPage}&search={}&status={}&wardId={}
> 
> 5. TABLE COLUMNS (ตาม schema):
>    - assetNo, name, wardId, status (badge สี), cost (formatCurrency), actions (edit/delete)
> 
> 6. STATE + FETCH:
>    - useState: assets[], loading, page, search, status, wardId
>    - useEffect: fetch เมื่อ search/status/wardId/page เปลี่ยน
>    - loading → show spinner
>    - error → show error message
>    - empty → show "ไม่พบข้อมูล"
> 
> ทำทั้งหมดในไฟล์เดียว เขียนโค้ดให้ครบ ไม่ต้องถาม
> ```

> The user says "ไม่มีการเขียนอะไรเลย" = "nothing is being written at all". So Continue (Qwen2.5 Coder 32B) said it's going to write the file, but actually nothing is being written. This is a common issue where the model "narrates" what it will do but doesn't actually produce the code/output.
> 
> This is a classic problem with AI coding assistants, especially reasoning models. The model talks about what it's going to do but doesn't actually execute/write.
> 
> Possible causes:
> 1. The model is in "chat" mode not "edit/apply" mode — so it just talks, doesn't write
> 2. maxTokens too low — it uses tokens on "thinking" and runs out before writing
> 3. The model just narrates without producing code blocks
> 4. Continue's "edit" vs "chat" mode issue
> 
> Solutions:
> 1. Make sure the model role includes "edit" and "apply" (it does in config)
> 2. In Continue, use the "Edit" mode or command that actually applies changes, not just chat
> 3. Ask it to output the FULL code explicitly
> 4. Use a more direct prompt: "output the complete file content"
> 
> The most practical fix: The user needs to either:
> - Use Continue's "edit file" command (not just chat)
> - Or ask the model to paste the full code, then apply manually
> - Or I just do it here and push
> 
> Given the repeated friction with Continue, I should strongly suggest Option B: I write the code here, push, DGX pulls. This is the most reliable path.
> 
> Let me be direct and helpful. Keep it concise.

> export interface CatalogProposal {
>   id: string;

>   category: string;

> 

#### _Assistant_

> Now I can see the current file. There are MAJOR problems:
> 
> 1. **Wrong field names** — the `Asset` interface uses `assetNumber`, `assetName`, `assetCategory`, `department`, `location`, `quantity`, `unitPrice`, `supplier`, `purchaseDate`, `fiscalYear`, `notes`, `createdAt` — NONE of these match the actual Prisma schema (`assetNo`, `name`, `wardId`, `status`, `cost`, `version`, `needsReview`)
>
